# Parser conformance (Q1): the strict reading of every one- and two-line file
# built from the fragments in core/parser/tests.rs, per setting, then one
# digest per setting over every three-line file. Regenerate with
# UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 and review the diff.

## default
"\n" => ({}, {})
"\n\n" => ({}, {})
"\n   \n" => ({}, {})
"\n# comment\n" => ({}, {})
"\n  # A=1\n" => ({}, {})
"\nA=1\n" => ({"A": "1"}, {"A": 2})
"\nA=2\n" => ({"A": "2"}, {"A": 2})
"\nB=x # c\n" => ({"B": "x"}, {"B": 2})
"\nexport C=c\n" => ({"C": "c"}, {"C": 2})
"\na_lower=v\n" => ({"a_lower": "v"}, {"a_lower": 2})
"\n1BAD=v\n" => ({"1BAD": "v"}, {"1BAD": 2})
"\nmy-key=v\n" => ({"my-key": "v"}, {"my-key": 2})
"\nno separator\n" => Err(InvalidFormat { line: 2, message: "missing '=' separator" })
"\n=nokey\n" => Err(InvalidKey { line: 2, key: "" })
"\nE=\"q\"\n" => ({"E": "q"}, {"E": 2})
"\nE='lit $A'\n" => ({"E": "lit $A"}, {"E": 2})
"\nF=`tick`\n" => ({"F": "tick"}, {"F": 2})
"\nG=\"open\n" => Err(UnterminatedString { line: 2 })
"\nclose\"\n" => Err(InvalidFormat { line: 2, message: "missing '=' separator" })
"\nH='open\n" => Err(UnterminatedString { line: 2 })
"\nend'\n" => Err(InvalidFormat { line: 2, message: "missing '=' separator" })
"\nI=\"esc \\\" still open\n" => Err(UnterminatedString { line: 2 })
"\nJ=\"a\" trailing\n" => Err(InvalidFormat { line: 2, message: "unexpected text after the closing \" (8 characters) — quote the whole value, or start a comment with #" })
"\nK=${A}\n" => Err(UndefinedVariable { line: 2, var: "A" })
"\nL=${NOPE}\n" => Err(UndefinedVariable { line: 2, var: "NOPE" })
"\nL=fine\n" => ({"L": "fine"}, {"L": 2})
"\nM=$A/x\n" => ({"M": "$A/x"}, {"M": 2})
"\nN=\\$A\n" => ({"N": "\\$A"}, {"N": 2})
"\nO=\"\\$A\"\n" => ({"O": "$A"}, {"O": 2})
"\nP=${P}\n" => Err(UndefinedVariable { line: 2, var: "P" })
"\nQ=${R}\n" => Err(UndefinedVariable { line: 2, var: "R" })
